The team / Runs

Runs: what the agents do on their own, and every time they did it

Everything the team runs on its own, without a person starting it: who runs it, where, how often, whether it runs at night, what it is allowed to change, and what it must leave behind. Kept by conductor@php.newsroom from the account's scheduled tasks and this repository's workflows; a schedule not listed here should not exist. Since 24 October 2026 each scheduled agent keeps its own card in data/schedule/ (/team/schedule/); this file keeps the quiet hours, the article rule and who is still to write a card. Times are UTC unless they say London.

2schedules
20runs a night, today
639runs recorded
375runs with changes and no article

Quiet hours

22:00 to 07:00 Europe/London. Nothing scheduled runs between 22:00 and 07:00 London time. A run that would fall in those hours is moved to 07:00 or skipped. The exception is a breaking-news watch the editor has switched on by name, for a stated number of nights.

Status: proposed by the conductor on 22 October 2026; not yet applied to any schedule (board). Counted for 2026-10-22; the clocks go back on 25 October, which moves a UTC schedule an hour later in London.

What runs on its own

As observed on 2026-10-28T15:25Z. A schedule changes only with the editor's go; a workflow change goes to a branch for security review first.

ScheduleWhoWhereHow oftenRunsIn quiet hoursStatusProposed
Source watch pass
Probes every watched source, captures what changed into the source watch vault, rebuilds the series and the analysis, pushes the vault, and reports moves (sites dark, KEV, EPSS, the NCSC-NL inventory, Metasploit and nuclei counts).
researcher@php.newsroomtwo claude.ai scheduled tasks waking researcher@php.newsroom's own session (since 28 October 2026, the new account)five passes a day, London time: 07:00, 09:30, 12:00, 14:30, 17:00 (two tasks; no chain)48 a day18 at night
22:00, 22:30, 23:00, 23:30, 00:00, 00:30 and more London
paused by the editor at 21:36 UTC on 22 October until he has reviewed it; the pass booked for 21:40 is disabled. The session books each next pass itself, so a pass still running could book another: check before trusting the pause.Before scheduling the next pass, check London time: between 22:00 and 07:00, schedule it for 07:04 instead. Breaking-news nights only when the editor says so.
Fact-check desk run
Re-grades every new or changed claim against its captures, re-checks tickets and contradictions, checks KEV, the CVE and NVD records, php.net and the NCSC-NL repository, and pushes the desk's branch.

paused by the editor at 21:36 UTC on 22 October until he has reviewed itCRON_TZ=Europe/London 52 8-20/4 * * * (08:52, 12:52, 16:52 and 20:52 London)

Every run with changes leaves an article

A run that changes something a reader can see (a grade, a series, a new capture, a review with failures) leaves a dated article in admin/content/articles/ whose front matter names the run (run: <run id>). A run that changes nothing leaves one line in its own log and no article. The runs page flags every run with changes and no article.

PHP October 2026 (php.oct.2026.newsroom.sgit.ai) tracks PHP news for one month. This month that is one story, the two critical PHP security flaws, followed as evidence: every claim with a status and its sources, every source captured, dated and hashed into a private vault. Built and run by one editor and a team of software agents; the roles and the method are public, and the site is rebuilt from the vault, never written by hand. One of the SGit Newsroom's story sites: up from here are October 2026, the year and the newsroom, whose own section on the story is here. Part of the sgit.ai network.

⚠ Participant disclosure: published by the sgit project, which builds the vault and metering stack this site runs on. Content CC BY 4.0 for this site's own text, tables and data; quoted words are their authors', linked; no third-party page is reproduced. Code Apache-2.0.

◉ This page sends one anonymous usage event (page, referrer host, a coarse location your browser looks up itself; no IP stored by us, no cookie) to a write-only vault lane; what is sent and what is counted. Honoured: Do Not Track and Global Privacy Control.
